Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf

Original source text:
The engine output shall not be less than that determined by the formula below and in no case less than 1,000 kW for ice class IA, IB and IC, and no less than 2,800 kW for IA Super.
Normalized requirement:
IF iceClass IN {'IA','IB','IC'}, THEN mcrPower_kW >= max(calculated_required_power_kW, 1000). IF iceClass = 'IA Super', THEN mcrPower_kW >= max(calculated_required_power_kW, 2800).
Required inputs: iceClass, mcrPower_kW

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltlgen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltlgen:PowerQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue unit:KiloW ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

nltlgen:TRF018ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                    nltl:iceClassIa
                    nltl:iceClassIb
                    nltl:iceClassIc
                ) ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "iceClass must be IA Super, IA, IB, or IC."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:calculatedRequiredPower ;
                sh:node nltlgen:PowerQuantityShape ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "calculatedRequiredPower must be supplied as a single kW quantity."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ;
                sh:node nltlgen:PowerQuantityShape ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "maximumContinuousRatingPower must be supplied as a single kW quantity."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                    sh:in (
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                        nltl:iceClassIa
                        nltl:iceClassIb
                        nltl:iceClassIc
                    )
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "maximumContinuousRatingPower must be at least the calculated required power and at least 1,000 kW for IA, IB, or IC, or 2,800 kW for IA Super." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:calculatedRequiredPower ?calculatedQuantity ;
                      nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?maximumQuantity .
                ?calculatedQuantity qudt:numericValue ?calculatedPower .
                ?maximumQuantity qudt:numericValue ?maximumPower .
                FILTER (
                    (
                        ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                        (?maximumPower < ?calculatedPower || ?maximumPower < 2800)
                    )
                    ||
                    (
                        (
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIa ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIb ||
                            ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIc
                        ) &&
                        (?maximumPower < ?calculatedPower || ?maximumPower < 1000)
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "maximumContinuousRatingPower must be at least the calculated required power and at least 1000 kW for IA, IB, or IC, or 2800 kW for IA Super." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                      nltl:calculatedRequiredPower ?calculatedQuantity ;
                      nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ?maximumQuantity .
                ?calculatedQuantity qudt:numericValue ?calculatedPower ;
                                    qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                ?maximumQuantity qudt:numericValue ?maximumPower ;
                                 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloW> .
                FILTER (
                    (
                        ?iceClass IN (
                            nltl:iceClassIa,
                            nltl:iceClassIb,
                            nltl:iceClassIc
                        )
                        && ?maximumPower < ?calculatedPower
                    )
                    ||
                    (
                        ?iceClass IN (
                            nltl:iceClassIa,
                            nltl:iceClassIb,
                            nltl:iceClassIc
                        )
                        && ?maximumPower < 1000
                    )
                    ||
                    (
                        ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                        && ?maximumPower < ?calculatedPower
                    )
                    ||
                    (
                        ?iceClass = nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                        && ?maximumPower < 2800
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
An ice-strengthened ship is assumed to operate in open sea conditions corresponding to a level ice thickness not exceeding hi. The design ice load height (h) of the area actually under ice pressure at any particular point of time is, however, assumed to be only a fraction of the ice thickness. The values for hi and h are given in Table 4-1. Table 4-1 values (ice class: hi, h in metres): IA Super: 1.0, 0.35; IA: 0.8, 0.30; IB: 0.6, 0.25; IC: 0.4, 0.22.

```turtle
@prefix nltlgen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltlgen:TRF036_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
        ) ;
        sh:message "The ship must have one applicable ice class from Table 4-1."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIceClassDesignParameterSet ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassDesignParameterSet ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node nltlgen:TRF036_ParameterSetShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide an ice-class design parameter set."
    ] .

nltlgen:TRF036_ParameterSetShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:iceClassDesignParameterSet ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:levelIceThickness ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Level ice thickness must be a single metre quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceLoadHeight ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Design ice load height must be a single metre quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The level ice thickness and design ice load height must match the Table 4-1 values for the ship's ice class." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                ?ship nltl:hasIceClassDesignParameterSet $this ;
                       nltl:iceClass ?class .
                $this nltl:levelIceThickness ?hiQuantity ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?hQuantity .
                ?hiQuantity qudt:numericValue ?hi ;
                            qudt:unit unit:M .
                ?hQuantity qudt:numericValue ?h ;
                           qudt:unit unit:M .
                FILTER (
                    (?class = nltl:iceClassIaSuper &&
                     (?hi > 1.0 || ?h != 0.35)) ||
                    (?class = nltl:iceClassIa &&
                     (?hi > 0.8 || ?h != 0.30)) ||
                    (?class = nltl:iceClassIb &&
                     (?hi > 0.6 || ?h != 0.25)) ||
                    (?class = nltl:iceClassIc &&
                     (?hi > 0.4 || ?h != 0.22))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIceClassDesignParameterSet ;

    a sh:NodeShape ;

        gen:IceClassIaSuperCaseShape
        gen:IceClassIaCaseShape
        gen:IceClassIbCaseShape

        sh:maxCount 1

        sh:minCount 1 ;
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE

Clause / section / page: 5.2.1.5 / Part II-A Chapter 5 - Garbage / 43-44
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-123-20260915T115123446018Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Discharge of cargo residues that cannot be recovered using commonly available methods for unloading shall only be permitted while the ship is en route and where all of the following conditions are satisfied: cargo residues, cleaning agents or additives, contained in hold washing water do not include any substances classified as harmful to the marine environment; both the port of departure and the next port of destination are within Arctic waters and the ship will not transit outside Arctic waters between those ports; no adequate reception facilities are available at those ports; and discharge of cargo hold washing water containing residues shall be made as far as practicable from areas of ice concentration exceeding 1/10, but in any case not less than 12 nautical miles from the nearest land, nearest ice shelf, or nearest fast ice.
Normalized requirement:
Cargo-residue discharge is permitted only if residue_not_recoverable_by_common_methods = true, ship_en_route = true, harmful_substance_present = false, departure_and_destination_within_Arctic = true, route_remains_within_Arctic = true, adequate_reception_facilities_available = false, and distance_to_nearest_land_ice_shelf_or_fast_ice_nm >= 12; record distance from ice concentration greater than 1/10.
Required inputs: Cargo/cleaning-agent classification, voyage route, reception-facility status, and discharge position/ice data

```turtle
gs:IMO123ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:residueNotRecoverableByCommonMethods ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Cargo residues must not be recoverable by commonly available unloading methods."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipEnRoute ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Discharge is permitted only while the ship is en route."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:harmfulSubstancePresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:message "Cargo residues, cleaning agents, or additives must not include substances harmful to the marine environment."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:departureAndDestinationWithinArctic ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The port of departure and next port of destination must both be within Arctic waters."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:routeRemainsWithinArctic ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship must not transit outside Arctic waters between the relevant ports."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:adequateReceptionFacilitiesAvailable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:message "No adequate reception facilities may be available at the relevant ports."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasDischargeDistanceRecord ;
        sh:class nltl:dischargeDistanceRecords ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:IMO123DischargeDistanceRecordShape ;
        sh:message "A discharge-distance record with both required distances must be provided."
    ] .
```

Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-031-20260915T103625575825Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Materials of exposed structures in ships shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account standards acceptable to the Organization or other standards offering an equivalent level of safety based on the polar service temperature.
Normalized requirement:
Every exposed-structure material shall have approval evidence from the Administration or an accepted recognized organization, and the approval basis shall reference the PST and an accepted or equivalent-safety standard.
Required inputs: Material records, approval certificate, approving authority, PST, and referenced standard

```turtle
<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-031-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;

        sh:path nltl:exposedStructureMaterial ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each applicable ship must identify exposed-structure material." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvingAuthority ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;

        ) ;

    ] ;

        ) ;
        sh:message "The approval standard must be Organization-acceptable or offer an equivalent level of safety." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:materialApprovalStatus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:message "Exposed-structure material approval must have approved status." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarServiceTemperature ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [

                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
            ] ;
        ] ;
        sh:message "The approval basis must preserve the ship's polar service temperature in degrees Celsius." ;
```

Original source text:
Category C ship means a ship designed to operate in open water or in ice conditions less severe than those included in categories A and B.
Normalized requirement:
IF a ship is designed for open water or ice conditions less severe than categories A and B, THEN ship_category = 'C'.
Required inputs: Ship design ice condition and declared Polar Code category

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO_003_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceConditionValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide its design ice condition to establish applicability of the category rule."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A ship designed for open water or ice conditions less severe than categories A and B must have ship category C." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designIceCondition ?condition .
                FILTER (
                    ?condition = nltl:openWaterIceCondition ||
                    ?condition = nltl:iceConditionLessSevereThanCategoryAAndB
                )
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:shipCategory nltl:polarShipCategoryC .
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:shipCategory ?category .
                        FILTER (?category != nltl:polarShipCategoryC)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gen:IMO003ShipDesignIceCategoryShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceConditionValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must declare its design ice condition so the Category C applicability branch can be determined."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:iceConditionValue ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:openWaterIceCondition
                    nltl:iceConditionLessSevereThanCategoryAAndB
                )
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:polarShipCategoryValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryC )
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:designIceCondition ?condition .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:designIceCondition ?applicableCondition .
                            FILTER (
                                ?applicableCondition IN (
                                    nltl:openWaterIceCondition,
                                    nltl:iceConditionLessSevereThanCategoryAAndB
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                """ ;
                sh:message "The declared design ice condition is not open water or less severe than categories A and B; the conditional Category C requirement does not apply."
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "When a ship is designed for open water or ice conditions less severe than categories A and B, its ship category must be Category C." .

```

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gs:IMO003ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:openWaterIceCondition
            nltl:iceConditionLessSevereThanCategoryAAndB
        ) ;
        sh:message "A ship shall declare a permitted design ice condition for this rule."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:openWaterIceCondition ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryC ) ;
                sh:message "A ship designed for open water shall have Polar Code category C."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:iceConditionLessSevereThanCategoryAAndB ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryC ) ;
                sh:message "A ship designed for ice conditions less severe than categories A and B shall have Polar Code category C."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Clause / section / page: 4.5.2 / Stringers outside the ice belt / 22-23
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-054-20260915T131545225696Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The section modulus of a stringer situated outside the ice belt but supporting ice-strengthened frames shall be calculated using Z = f_9*f_10*p*h*l^2/(m*sigma_y)*(1 - h_s/l_s)*10^6 [cm^3] (4.13), and the effective shear area shall be A = sqrt(3)*f_9*f_10*f_11*p*h*l/(2*sigma_y)*(1 - h_s/l_s)*10^4 [cm^2] (4.14). Use p from 4.2.2, h from 4.2.1, p*h = 0.15 MN/m when the calculated product is less than 0.15 MN/m, l as stringer span, m from 4.4.3, l_s as the distance to the adjacent ice stringer, h_s as the distance to the ice belt, f_9 = 0.80, f_10 = 1.8, f_11 = 1.2, and sigma_y from 4.3.2.
Normalized requirement:
Set design_line_load_MN_m = max(p_MPa*h_m, 0.15) and outside_ice_belt_factor = 1 - h_s_m/l_s_m. Compute Z_cm3 = 0.80*1.8*design_line_load_MN_m*l_m^2/(m*sigma_y_N_mm2)*outside_ice_belt_factor*10^6 and A_cm2 = sqrt(3)*0.80*1.8*1.2*design_line_load_MN_m*l_m/(2*sigma_y_N_mm2)*outside_ice_belt_factor*10^4.
Required inputs: yieldStrength_MPa, sectionModulusZ, shearAreaA

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                      nltl:supportsIceStrengthenedFrames true ;
                      nltl:icePressure/qudt:numericValue ?p ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight/qudt:numericValue ?h ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorM/qudt:numericValue ?m ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength/qudt:numericValue ?sy ;
                      nltl:distanceToIceBelt/qudt:numericValue ?hs ;
                      nltl:distanceToAdjacentIceStringer/qudt:numericValue ?ls ;

                      nltl:sectionModulus/qudt:numericValue ?reportedZ ;
                      nltl:requiredShearArea/qudt:numericValue ?reportedA .

                BIND(IF(?product < 0.15, 0.15, ?product) AS ?loadMNPerM)
                BIND(1 - (?hs / ?ls) AS ?factor)
                BIND(0.8 * 1.8 * ?loadMNPerM * ?l * ?l /
                     (?m * ?sy) * ?factor * 1000000 AS ?expectedZ)
                BIND(1.7320508075688772 * 0.8 * 1.8 * 1.2 *
                     ?loadMNPerM * ?l / (2 * ?sy) * ?factor * 10000 AS ?expectedA)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?reportedLoad - (?loadMNPerM * 1000)) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?loadMNPerM * 1000) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?reportedFactor - ?factor) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?factor) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?reportedZ - ?expectedZ) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedZ) + 0.000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?reportedA - ?expectedA) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedA) + 0.000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
    sh:property [

    sh:property [

        sh:minCount 1 ;

    ] ;
    sh:property [

        sh:node gen:AreaQuantity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;

        sh:message "requiredShearArea is required in cm2."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "designLineLoad, outsideIceBeltFactor, sectionModulus, and requiredShearArea must satisfy the TRF-054 formulas; derived decimal results use a relative tolerance of 1e-6 with a 1e-12 absolute fallback." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this
                    nltl:icePressure ?pressureNode ;
                    nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?heightNode ;
                    nltl:span ?spanNode ;
                    nltl:stringerSpan ?stringerSpanNode ;
                    nltl:frameMomentFactorM ?momentNode ;
                    nltl:yieldStrength ?yieldNode ;
                    nltl:distanceToIceBelt ?iceBeltNode ;
                    nltl:distanceToAdjacentIceStringer ?adjacentNode ;
                    nltl:designLineLoad ?lineLoadNode ;
                    nltl:outsideIceBeltFactor ?outsideFactorNode ;
                    nltl:sectionModulus ?sectionNode ;
                    nltl:requiredShearArea ?areaNode .

                ?pressureNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?heightNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?spanNode qudt:numericValue ?span .
                ?stringerSpanNode qudt:numericValue ?l .
                ?momentNode qudt:numericValue ?m .
                ?yieldNode qudt:numericValue ?sigma .
                ?iceBeltNode qudt:numericValue ?hs .
                ?adjacentNode qudt:numericValue ?ls .
                ?lineLoadNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedLine .
                ?outsideFactorNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedFactor .
                ?sectionNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedZ .
                ?areaNode qudt:numericValue ?reportedA .

                BIND(IF((?p * ?h) < 0.15, 0.15, (?p * ?h)) AS ?line)
                BIND((1.0 - (?hs / ?ls)) AS ?outsideFactor)
                BIND((0.80 * 1.8 * ?line * ?l * ?l / (?m * ?sigma) * ?outsideFactor * 1000000.0) AS ?z)
                BIND((1.7320508075688772 * 0.80 * 1.8 * 1.2 * ?line * ?l / (2.0 * ?sigma) * ?outsideFactor * 10000.0) AS ?a)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?reportedLine - ?line) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?line) > 0.000000000001, ABS(?line), 0.000000000001))
                    ||
                    ABS(?reportedFactor - ?outsideFactor) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?outsideFactor) > 0.000000000001, ABS(?outsideFactor), 0.000000000001))
                    ||
                    ABS(?reportedZ - ?z) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?z) > 0.000000000001, ABS(?z), 0.000000000001))
                    ||
                    ABS(?reportedA - ?a) >
                    (0.000001 * IF(ABS(?a) > 0.000000000001, ABS(?a), 0.000000000001))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                $this nltl:icePressure ?pNode ;
                      nltl:designIceLoadHeight ?hNode ;
                      nltl:frameMomentFactorM ?mNode ;
                      nltl:yieldStrength ?yNode ;
                      nltl:distanceToIceBelt ?hsNode ;
                      nltl:stringerSpan ?lsNode ;
                      nltl:designLineLoad ?lineNode ;
                      nltl:outsideIceBeltFactor ?factorNode ;
                      nltl:sectionModulus ?zNode ;

                ?pNode qudt:numericValue ?p .
                ?hNode qudt:numericValue ?h .
                ?mNode qudt:numericValue ?m .
                ?yNode qudt:numericValue ?y .
                ?hsNode qudt:numericValue ?hs .
                ?lsNode qudt:numericValue ?ls .
                ?lineNode qudt:numericValue ?line .
                ?factorNode qudt:numericValue ?factor .
                ?zNode qudt:numericValue ?z .
                ?aNode qudt:numericValue ?a .

                BIND((IF((?p * ?h) < 0.15, 0.15, (?p * ?h))) AS ?expectedLine)
                BIND((1 - (?hs / ?ls)) AS ?expectedFactor)
                BIND((0.80 * 1.8 * ?expectedLine * (?ls * ?ls) /
                      (?m * ?y) * ?expectedFactor * 1000000) AS ?expectedZ)
                BIND((1.7320508075688772 * 0.80 * 1.8 * 1.2 * ?expectedLine * ?ls /
                      (2 * ?y) * ?expectedFactor * 10000) AS ?expectedA)

                FILTER(
                    ABS(?line - ?expectedLine) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedLine) + 0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?factor - ?expectedFactor) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedFactor) + 0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?z - ?expectedZ) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedZ) + 0.000000001)
                    ||
                    ABS(?a - ?expectedA) >
                    (0.000001 * ABS(?expectedA) + 0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
```
```turtle
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
```

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-077-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A single propeller type must identify the applicable ice-force distribution branch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceLoadCycleCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The number of ice-load cycles must be recorded as an integer."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeIceLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The blade ice load must be a single force quantity in kilonewtons."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumBladeIceLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The maximum blade ice load must be a single force quantity in kilonewtons."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:hasValue "open propeller" ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadSpectrumShapeParameter ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:minInclusive "0.75"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "0.75"^^xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:hasValue "ducted propeller blade" ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadSpectrumShapeParameter ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:minInclusive "1.0"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "1.0"^^xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The blade ice load must satisfy 0 <= Fice <= maximumBladeIceLoad." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeIceLoad ?loadQuantity .
                ?loadQuantity qudt:numericValue ?loadValue .
                $this nltl:maximumBladeIceLoad ?maximumQuantity .
                ?maximumQuantity qudt:numericValue ?maximumValue .
                FILTER (
                    ?loadValue < 0 ||
                    ?maximumValue < 0 ||
                    ?loadValue > ?maximumValue
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-077-ShipShape>

    ] ;

        sh:path nltl:bladeIceLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;

                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;

                sh:minInclusive "0"^^xsd:decimal
            ] ;

                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Blade ice load must be a non-negative value in kilonewtons."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumBladeIceLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minInclusive "0"^^xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloN
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The maximum blade ice load must be a non-negative value in kilonewtons."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:hasValue "open propeller"
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadSpectrumShapeParameter ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minInclusive "0.75"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "0.75"^^xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:hasValue "ducted propeller blade"
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:loadSpectrumShapeParameter ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minInclusive "1.0"^^xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "1.0"^^xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The blade ice load must not exceed the maximum blade ice load." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeIceLoad ?iceLoad .
                $this nltl:maximumBladeIceLoad ?maximumLoad .
                ?iceLoad qudt:numericValue ?iceValue .
                ?iceLoad qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                ?maximumLoad qudt:numericValue ?maximumValue .
                ?maximumLoad qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                FILTER (?iceValue > ?maximumValue)
            }
        """
    ] .
```
